## 2026-10-23 M0.2/A10 wheel payload completeness

- **Gap:** `qa/wheel_manifest.py` compared only `.py` payloads. A wheel could omit `py.typed` or other package data and still pass; it also accepted a wheel whose METADATA version differed from `pyproject.toml`, and duplicate ZIP payload paths were collapsed by a dictionary.
- **Change:** Wheel verification now compares every regular file under each package `src/` tree, including `py.typed`, with installed wheel payload files. It excludes generated `__pycache__`, bytecode, `.egg-info`, `.dist-info` and `.data` metadata paths; it rejects version drift and duplicate payload names. The report schema is `rles.wheelhouse-manifest.v2` with distinct source/payload/Python file counts. Added regressions for missing `py.typed`, generated egg-info, wrong metadata version and duplicate wheel entries.

## 2026-10-23 F7 streamed detail artifact publication race

- **Finding:** The new streamed account/reconciliation JSONL output created an exclusive empty destination and then replaced it with the completed temporary file. A concurrent writer could swap the destination after the exclusive create and have its file overwritten by `os.replace`, so the implementation did not meet its no-overwrite claim.
- **Change:** Publish the completed same-directory temporary file with `os.link`, which atomically fails when the destination already exists, then remove the temporary name. Added a regression that inserts a concurrent destination immediately before publication and proves its contents survive while no partial artifact is left.
- **Verification:** Focused report row output tests — **2 passed**; report/account-row/rollout CLI selectors across research metrics, online accumulator parity, and application CLI — **51 passed, 104 deselected**. Ruff passed for current modified Python source/test files; Windows MyPy passed for `metrics.py`, `native_metrics.py`, and orchestrator `application.py`; `git diff --check` passed. Source manifest v3 regenerated and self-verified **370 files**, identity `d86f9c2c7ccc59f7c7d9ea224023447ba99b7056b9e431d90c966c866b5f0f0b`. One initial run used the restricted system Temp and failed during setup; rerunning with a workspace-local `TMP/TEMP` completed successfully.

## 2026-10-23 RouteB deep-audit remediation (RB-01/RB-02/RB-03/RB-06/RB-07/RB-08/RB-13/RB-14)

- **Baseline recovery:** Corrected the tracked legacy inventory marker to the exact LF-byte SHA-256 and added a byte-for-byte regression. Research-access tests pass **91/91**.
- **Fold protocol:** Added one shared half-open Validation anchor generator. `score_end` is exclusive, so the last anchor is `score_end-(H+1)m`. Added 1/5/15/16/60-minute coverage. Full protocol validation, including `volatility_window <= max_horizon`, now runs before output creation or fresh registered Fold consumption.
- **B3 semantics and data:** The F2 first step now applies the trained next-minute head to the final observed posterior; the first prior state is used only for the following step. Added a time-semantics truth table and exported posterior/prior heads for numerical checks. Checkpoints bind the original Train tensor SHA separately from the authorized inference-context SHA. Three-scale scalers and minute/hour/day loss masks now use an explicit Train-score half-open interval; slow buckets crossing the warmup boundary are state-only.
- **Formal probability score:** Added batched E1/E2 one-step distribution worker commands, artifact validation and Fold wiring. The formal E1/E2 report now supplies finite Validation-anchor return NLL values rather than `None`.
- **Checkpoint recovery:** Each save first publishes an immutable generation pair, then refreshes the conventional NPZ/JSON pair. Load falls back to the newest complete matching generation after a torn/corrupt convenience pair, retains the newest two generations, and still fails closed on duplicate-key manifests.
- **F2 boundary:** Hour/day decoder outputs are now explicitly named latent diagnostics and recorded as not being market OHLCV. Per-path minute sampling/aggregation conservation remains a separate open requirement; no economic or market-quality claim is made.
- **Verification:** Main RouteB/world-model + research suite **237 passed, 3 JAX-only skipped**; isolated Python 3.12.10/JAX 0.11.2 CPU suite **131 passed**; B2 and B3 CPU smokes **PASS**; B3 has nonzero gradients on all three scales, resume parity, posterior-head first-step equality, prior-state distinction, independent inference-context loading and causal slow-event behavior. Isolated MyPy passed **38 source files**; Ruff passed. Source manifest verifies **372 files**, identity `d9fdbb9a100bff7db20e539a3b308fa228ddc25af908958e70772eb8d39cf537`. Evidence: `docs/audit_evidence/20261023/routeb_audit_remediation_20261023.json` and `routeb_audit_remediation_b3_cpu_20261023.json`.
- **Open cursor:** RB-05 registered E3/E4 orchestration, RB-09 30/90/360-day profiling/slow-stream training optimization and RB-12 real calibration remain implementable work. RB-04 per-path conserved F2 OHLCV, RB-10 real open-loop distribution-shift evidence and RB-11 Nautilus-native economic parity require further implementation/evidence. Hosted Linux/Windows/JAX results for the exact remediation commit are still pending.

## 2026-10-24 RB-04 per-path OHLCV and slow-feature handoff

- **Change:** Added `sample_hierarchical_ohlcv_paths`: it samples per-minute diagonal-Gaussian feature paths, decodes with `OHLCVCodec` without clipping, counts/retries invalid draws under a fixed attempt cap, and returns each accepted OHLCV path. Completed UTC hour/day candles are recomputed from the same path plus its observed prefix; each bucket is independently re-aggregated and checked field-for-field. The result also carries hour/day feature vectors normalized by the frozen Train scalers, keyed by exact close timestamps, for a downstream slow-RSSM feedback update.
- **Verification:** New per-path sampler tests plus hierarchy clock tests: **11 passed**. Coverage includes a future hour crossing the anchor, a future UTC day crossing the anchor, exact OHLCV conservation, and all-invalid-path rejection accounting. Python compile and Ruff passed.
- **Boundary:** The sampler and feedback-feature handoff are not yet wired into a stateful per-path B3 worker; slow RSSM updates must consume those aggregate-derived features before E4's following minute forecast. Registered Fold E3/E4 remains gated, and no Validation/market/economic claim is made. RB-04 and RB-05 remain open.
- **Hosted state:** The latest fully hosted commit is still `8e6207b89fad6570e0b9ec18545736f25255b39c`; this path-enabled smoke change awaits the next CI run.

## 2026-10-24 RB-04 causal path-feedback replay

- **Change:** Added alignment of generated hour/day feedback features to the first future decision at or after each frozen availability barrier. B3 feedback replay now observes the already sampled minute feature at every step and observes path-derived hour/day aggregate features only at their scheduled events. The smoke compares E3/E4 over the same generated path ensemble.

The full pinned-upstream B3 CPU smoke passed locally under Python 3.12.10/JAX 0.11.2: 2 optimizer updates, nonzero gradients at all three scales, resume parity, valid 4×5×5 minute paths, exact slow-bar conservation, aligned feedback replay, and E3/E4 divergence after the slow event. Zero paths were rejected in this synthetic fixture. Detailed result: `docs/audit_evidence/20261023/routeb_audit_remediation_b3_feedback_cpu_20261024.json`.

No real-market, Validation, or economic claim is made. RB-04/RB-05 remain open; Hosted CI for this revision is pending.

## 2026-10-24 RB-04 recursive OHLCV generation reference

- **Gap addressed:** The previous sampler plus posterior replay did not provide a causal autoregressive generator in which each sampled minute candle is available to the next forecast. Consequently, it was not yet a complete input contract for registering E3/E4 path scoring.
- **Change:** Added a correctness-first recursive path orchestration API. It forecasts one minute, decodes and samples a legal OHLCV bar with bounded rejection accounting, appends its standardized minute feature to that path, recomputes completed UTC hour/day aggregates, and exposes slow feedback only at its scheduled availability decision. Added an F2 wrapper deriving event schedules from the frozen observed clock and replaying the selected E3/E4 mode. Added tests for stepwise feedback, delayed aggregate visibility, fail-closed event alignment and F2 wrapper wiring; extended the B3 smoke to exercise a short recursive E4 forecast.

Windows/WSL canonical guard tests — **3 passed on each platform**. The WSL smoke used DreamerV3 `e01491fad6434b2245a3b8ca201dd7faedcc458c`; the result is recorded at `docs/audit_evidence/20261023/routeb_recursive_b3_cpu_smoke_20261024.json`. Ruff passed on changed source/test/smoke files; MyPy passed on both changed source modules with missing optional JAX stubs ignored (and the pure NumPy path module without that option); `git diff --check` passed.
- **Performance and evidence boundary:** The reference F2 predictor reloads/replays the prefix at every step; this intentionally favors causal correctness and is not the stateful/performance implementation needed for long horizons. No RB-09 performance claim, registered Fold E3/E4 Validation score, real-market result or economic claim is made. Synthetic test fixtures only; no Validation/Test/Holdout data accessed. RB-04/RB-05 remain open pending a stateful worker/scoring integration and hosted verification.
